For the past few months, usually there’ll be one or two nights where I eat way more than I meant to, and then the next day I try to make up for it.\n\nI don’t always call it “skipping meals,” I guess—I’ll tell myself I’m just being careful or getting back on track—but it can be skipping breakfast or lunch, or exercising longer than I normally would. Does that answer what you’re asking?\n\nDoctor: Have you ever used laxatives after one of these episodes of eating more than you meant to?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. I mean, that’s not really something I’ve done after eating too much.\n\nI mostly just try to be careful the next day, but I haven’t used laxatives for that. Is that… the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Do you ever make yourself vomit after eating more than you meant to?\n\nPatient: No… I mean, I don’t make myself throw up after that. I know that’s something people ask about, and it’s kind of embarrassing to even talk about, but no, that’s not something I do.\n\nDoctor: How many days a week do you try to make up for eating more than you meant to by skipping meals or exercising more?\n\nPatient: Usually maybe one or two days a week… I mean, it’s not every day. It’s more like if I have one or two nights where I eat way more than I planned, then the next day I’ll try to “balance it out” by skipping a meal or exercising longer.\n\nThat’s been happening most weeks for the past few months, I guess. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Do you try to make up for eating more than you meant to every time it happens?\n\nPatient: Not every single time, no… or at least I try not to think of it that way. But a lot of the time, the next day I’ll tell myself I’m just getting back on track, like skipping breakfast and lunch or taking a longer hike than usual.\n\nSometimes with softball conditioning I’ll push harder too, even if I’m tired. I guess in my head I’m sort of “making up for it,” but I don’t know if that counts every time.\n\nDoctor: Do you try to make up for eating more than you meant to by skipping meals or exercising more every week?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but for the past few months it’s been most weeks. Usually there’ll be one or two nights where I eat way more than I meant to, and then the next day I’ll try to sort of “fix it” by skipping meals or exercising longer.\n\nIt’s not every day or anything, but… yeah, most weeks.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
